# Prim.Machine

Leaf inline-LLVM scalar machine primitives (fixed-width wrapping/saturating/overflow/bitwise/shift/rotate/bitscan/comparison/conversion operations and word-size bridges), re-exported through Data.Machine and shared directly by Data.Char, Data.Bytes, and Data.String.

<div class="doc-mod-bar"><div class="doc-mod-bar-primary"><span class="support-tag support-tag--current">cinch/stdlib</span> <code>import Prim.Machine</code></div><div class="doc-mod-bar-links"><a href="/packages/stdlib/Prim.Machine/source/">View source</a><a href="/packages/search/?q=%2BPrim.Machine">Search module</a><a href="/reference-sources/Prim.Machine.cinch" download="Prim.Machine.cinch">Download .cinch</a><a href="/packages/stdlib/">All stdlib modules</a></div></div>

**Dependencies:** [Algebra.Arithmetic](Algebra.Arithmetic.md).

<details class="doc-notes"><summary>Module notes</summary>

Leaf machine scalar primitives over fixed-width integers, pointer-width bridges, and floats.
Every operation lowers through an explicit inline LLVM `prim` fragment so neither
compiler maintains an independent `cinch.prim.*` scalar lowering table. Re-exported
through Data.Machine and shared directly by Data.Char, Data.Bytes, and Data.String.

</details>

<article class="doc-entry" id="v-raw-div-u8">
<div class="doc-sig-bar"><code class="doc-sig"><strong class="doc-decl-name tok-variable">raw-div-u8</strong> :: <a class="doc-type-link tok-constructor" href="/packages/stdlib/Data.Machine/" title="U8">U8</a> <span class="tok-operator">-&gt;</span> <span class="tok-variable">refined</span> (<span class="tok-variable">r</span> /= 0) <a class="doc-type-link tok-constructor" href="/packages/stdlib/Data.Machine/" title="U8">U8</a> <span class="tok-operator">-&gt;</span> <a class="doc-type-link tok-constructor" href="/packages/stdlib/Data.Machine/" title="U8">U8</a></code><span class="doc-sig-links"><a href="#v-raw-div-u8" title="Permalink">#</a><a href="/packages/stdlib/Prim.Machine/source/#L972" title="View source at line 972">Source</a></span></div>
<div class="doc-body">

Unsigned division/remainder: `refined (r /= 0)` rules out LLVM immediate UB
(`%1 == 0`), so no `unsafe` trust boundary is needed.

</div>
</article>

<article class="doc-entry" id="v-raw-sdiv-i8">
<div class="doc-sig-bar"><code class="doc-sig"><strong class="doc-decl-name tok-variable">raw-sdiv-i8</strong> :: (<span class="tok-variable">a</span> :: <a class="doc-type-link tok-constructor" href="/packages/stdlib/Data.Machine/" title="I8">I8</a>) <span class="tok-operator">-&gt;</span> <span class="tok-variable">refined</span> (<span class="tok-variable">r</span> /= 0 &amp;&amp; <span class="tok-constructor">Not</span> (<span class="tok-variable">a</span> == - 128 &amp;&amp; <span class="tok-variable">r</span> == - 1)) <a class="doc-type-link tok-constructor" href="/packages/stdlib/Data.Machine/" title="I8">I8</a> <span class="tok-operator">-&gt;</span> <a class="doc-type-link tok-constructor" href="/packages/stdlib/Data.Machine/" title="I8">I8</a></code><span class="doc-sig-links"><a href="#v-raw-sdiv-i8" title="Permalink">#</a><a href="/packages/stdlib/Prim.Machine/source/#L254" title="View source at line 254">Source</a></span></div>
<div class="doc-body">

Signed division/remainder: precondition rules out LLVM immediate UB
(`%1 == 0` or `%0 == MIN && %1 == -1`), so no `unsafe` trust boundary is needed.

</div>
</article>
